     return (json.dumps(_json_safe(value), sort_keys=True, indent=2, allow_nan=False)+"\n").encode()
 
 
+def _json_chunks(value, depth=0):
+    """Canonical JSON text without copying the result tree or buffering output.
+
+    Memory is bounded by active object key sorts, nesting and the largest integer
+    token. Strings (including keys) are escaped in bounded character chunks.
+    A caller must not mutate containers while validating/writing them.
+    """
+    if isinstance(value, Fraction):
+        value = {"numerator": value.numerator, "denominator": value.denominator}
+    elif isinstance(value, Path):
+        value = str(value)
+    if isinstance(value, str):
+        yield '"'
+        for offset in range(0, len(value), 8192):
+            yield json.dumps(value[offset:offset+8192], ensure_ascii=True)[1:-1]
+        yield '"'
+    elif value is None or isinstance(value, (bool, Integral, float)):
+        if isinstance(value, Integral) and not isinstance(value, bool):
+            value = int(value)
+        if isinstance(value, float) and not math.isfinite(value):
+            raise ValueError("nonfinite result value")
+        yield json.dumps(value, allow_nan=False)
+    elif isinstance(value, (tuple, list, dict)):
+        mapping = isinstance(value, dict)
+        if mapping:
+            keys = {}
+            for key in value:
+                if not isinstance(key, (str, int)) or isinstance(key, bool):
+                    raise TypeError("JSON object keys must be strings/integers")
+                text = str(key)
+                if text in keys:
+                    raise ValueError("JSON key normalization collision")
+                keys[text] = key
+            entries = sorted(keys)
+        else:
+            entries = range(len(value))
+        yield "{" if mapping else "["
+        for position, key in enumerate(entries):
+            yield (",\n" if position else "\n") + "  "*(depth+1)
+            if mapping:
+                yield from _json_chunks(key, depth+1)
+                yield ": "
+                item = value[keys[key]]
+            else:
+                item = value[key]
+            yield from _json_chunks(item, depth+1)
+        if len(value):
+            yield "\n" + "  "*depth
+        yield "}" if mapping else "]"
+    else:
+        raise TypeError(f"unsupported exact result type: {type(value).__name__}")
+
+
+def _validate_json(value):
+    # Consume the same encoder before creating either artifact: late/deep bad
+    # values and integer encoding errors must not produce successful artifacts.
+    for _ in _json_chunks(value):
+        pass
+
+
+def _write_json(path, value):
+    """Exclusively create canonical UTF-8 JSON and hash actual bytes written."""
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open("xb") as stream:
+        for chunk in _json_chunks(value):
+            data = chunk.encode("utf-8")
+            if stream.write(data) != len(data):
+                raise OSError("short artifact write")
+            digest.update(data)
+        if stream.write(b"\n") != 1:
+            raise OSError("short artifact write")
+        digest.update(b"\n")
+    return digest.hexdigest()
+
+
+def _file_sha256(path):
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with path.open("rb") as stream:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024*1024), b""):
+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()
+

+    def test_stream_exact_canonical_bytes_and_manifest_snapshot(self):
+        class ExactInteger(IntEnum):
+            VALUE = 23
+        result = self.result()
+        result["parameters"].update({"fraction": Fraction(-7, 13), "path": Path("é/x")})
+        result["checks"]["tuple"] = (ExactInteger.VALUE, Fraction(2, 5))
+        result["cases"] = [{10: [None, True, False, -0.0, 1e-90],
+                            2: (2**521, -(2**523), ExactInteger.VALUE),
+                            "unicode": '\"\\\b\n\t é𝄞\ud800'*9000,
+                            "empty": ({}, [], ()), "path": Path("é/x")}]
+        before = copy.deepcopy(result)
+        expected = (json.dumps(core._json_safe(result), sort_keys=True, indent=2,
+                               allow_nan=False)+"\n").encode()
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="lattice-core-softwarecheck-") as temporary:
+            directory = Path(temporary)
+            original = core._json_safe
+            def selected_only(value):
+                self.assertIsNot(value, result, "must not normalize entire raw result")
+                self.assertIsNot(value, result["cases"], "must not move the raw clone")
+                return original(value)
+            with patch.object(core, "_json_bytes", side_effect=AssertionError("whole output")):
+                with patch.object(core, "_json_safe", side_effect=selected_only):
+                    manifest = self.write(directory, result)
+            self.assertEqual((directory/"raw-result.json").read_bytes(), expected)
+            self.assertEqual(manifest, json.loads((directory/"manifest.yaml").read_text()))
+            self.assertEqual((directory/"manifest.yaml").read_bytes(), core._json_bytes(manifest))
+            self.assertEqual(result, before)
+            result["parameters"]["quadratic"] = (99, 99)
+            result["checks"]["tuple"] = ()
+            self.assertEqual(manifest["run"]["inputs"]["parameters"]["quadratic"], [2, 0])
+            self.assertEqual(manifest["run"]["result"]["checks"]["tuple"],
+                             [23, {"numerator": 2, "denominator": 5}])
+            with patch.object(Path, "read_bytes", side_effect=AssertionError("whole hash buffer")):
+                self.assertTrue(check_artifacts(directory)["envelope_valid"])
+
+    def test_deep_late_invalid_values_keys_and_collisions(self):
+        for bad in (object(), {True: 1}, {(1,): 2}, {1: 1, "1": 2},
+                    float("nan"), float("inf"), -float("inf")):
+            with self.subTest(bad=type(bad).__name__):
+                with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="lattice-core-softwarecheck-") as temporary:
+                    result = self.result()
+                    result["zz_late"] = [dict(valid=list(range(200))), {"deep": [bad]}]
+                    with self.assertRaises((TypeError, ValueError)):
+                        self.write(Path(temporary), result)
+                    self.assertFalse((Path(temporary)/"raw-result.json").exists())
+                    self.assertFalse((Path(temporary)/"manifest.yaml").exists())

+    def test_symlink_and_existing_manifest_refuse_without_overwrite(self):
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="lattice-core-softwarecheck-") as temporary:
+            root = Path(temporary)
+            target = root/"target"
+            target.mkdir()
+            alias = root/"alias"
+            alias.symlink_to(target, target_is_directory=True)
+            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "symlink"):
+                self.write(alias)
+            for name in ("raw-result.json", "manifest.yaml"):
+                directory = root/name.replace(".", "-")
+                directory.mkdir()
+                destination = directory/name
+                destination.symlink_to(root/"absent")
+                with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):
+                    self.write(directory)
+                self.assertTrue(destination.is_symlink())
+                self.assertFalse((root/"absent").exists())
+            directory = root/"existing"
+            directory.mkdir()
+            (directory/"manifest.yaml").write_bytes(b"preserved")
+            with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):
+                self.write(directory)
+            self.assertEqual((directory/"manifest.yaml").read_bytes(), b"preserved")
+            self.assertFalse((directory/"raw-result.json").exists())

+    def test_invalid_timing_seed_and_provenance_before_success(self):
+        # Model time spent preparing normalized protocol snapshots and writing
+        # artifacts separately: the published preparation timer includes the
+        # former and excludes streaming encoding/hash/I/O.
+        result = self.result()
+        clock = [5.0]
+        normalize, validate, write = core._json_safe, core._validate_json, core._write_json
+        def normalize_snapshot(value):
+            if value is result["parameters"] or value is result["checks"]:
+                clock[0] += 3.0
+            return normalize(value)
+        def validate_payload(value):
+            clock[0] += 2.0 if value is result else 7.0
+            return validate(value)
+        def write_payload(*args):
+            clock[0] += 100.0
+            return write(*args)
+        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="lattice-core-softwarecheck-") as temporary:
+            with patch.object(core, "_json_safe", side_effect=normalize_snapshot), \
+                 patch.object(core, "_validate_json", side_effect=validate_payload), \
+                 patch.object(core, "_write_json", side_effect=write_payload), \
+                 patch.object(core.time, "monotonic", side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), \
+                 patch.object(core.time, "process_time", side_effect=lambda: clock[0]):
+                manifest = write_artifacts(result, temporary, 7, "2026-10-16T00:00:00Z", 0, 0)
+            self.assertEqual(manifest["run"]["timing"]["wall_seconds"], 13.0)
+            self.assertEqual(manifest["run"]["timing"]["process_cpu_seconds"], 13.0)
+        for seed, started, wall, cpu in ((True, "2026-10-16T00:00:00Z", 0, 0),
+                                       (7, "bad", 0, 0),
+                                       (7, "2026-10-16T00:00:00Z", float("inf"), 0),
+                                       (7, "2026-10-16T00:00:00Z", 0, float("inf"))):
+            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="lattice-core-softwarecheck-") as temporary:
+                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):

RECONSTRUCTION INPUT stress.py
import gc, hashlib, importlib.machinery, importlib.util, json, os, resource, sys, time, traceback
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from fractions import Fraction
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path('/tmp/lattice-serialization-source-282fd3')
mode, variant, name = sys.argv[1:]
frozen=json.loads((OUT/'frozen-fixture.json').read_text())
fixture=next(x for x in frozen['workloads'] if x['name']==name)
source = OUT/'baseline-source.stdout' if variant=='baseline' else Path('/workspace/lattice-index-calculus/tools/lattice_ic_core.py')
loader=importlib.machinery.SourceFileLoader('control_core',str(source))
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_loader(loader.name,loader)
core=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);loader.exec_module(core)
directory=OUT/(variant+'-'+name)
N=fixture['rows']
normalized={'exact':{'numerator':-7,'denominator':13},'path':'synthetic/é','tuple':[2**160,-2**161,None,True,-0.0],'nested':[{}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}]}
parameters={'fixture':'synthetic-shared-nested-v1','rows':N}
checks={'synthetic':True}
record={'mode':mode,'variant':variant,'fixture':fixture,'source_sha256':hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'frozen_fixture_sha256':hashlib.sha256((OUT/'frozen-fixture.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'guard':{'RLIMIT_AS':resource.getrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_AS),'OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS':os.environ.get('OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS'),'workers':1},'native_peak_rss_unit':'KiB','rss_scope':'RUSAGE_SELF lifetime high-water including setup, imports, resident ballast and serialization or parser; excludes other subprocesses'}
try:
 if mode=='write':
  row={'exact':Fraction(-7,13),'path':Path('synthetic/é'),'tuple':(2**160,-2**161,None,True,-0.0),'nested':[{}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}]}
  result={'softwarecheck':True,'parameters':parameters,'checks':checks,'cases':[row]*N}
  ballast=bytearray(fixture['resident_ballast_bytes'])
  wall,cpu=time.monotonic(),time.process_time()
  try:
   manifest=core.write_artifacts(result,directory,7,datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),wall,cpu)
  finally:
   record.update(wall_seconds=time.monotonic()-wall,cpu_seconds=time.process_time()-cpu,native_peak_rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss)
  del ballast,result;gc.collect()
  raw=directory/'raw-result.json'
  # Independent canonical oracle: literal scaffolding from stdlib normalized
  # small object, and one separately rendered row repeated, no candidate encoder.
  row_bytes=('\n'.join('    '+line for line in json.dumps(normalized,sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False).splitlines())).encode()
  outer=(json.dumps({'softwarecheck':True,'parameters':parameters,'checks':checks,'cases':[]},sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode()
  prefix,suffix=outer.split(b'[]',1)
  oracle=hashlib.sha256();size=0
  def feed(b):
   global size
   oracle.update(b);size+=len(b)
  feed(prefix+b'[\n')
  for i in range(N):
   if i:feed(b',\n')
   feed(row_bytes)
  feed(b'\n  ]'+suffix)
  actual=core._file_sha256(raw) if hasattr(core,'_file_sha256') else hashlib.file_digest(raw.open('rb'),'sha256').hexdigest()
  assert actual==oracle.hexdigest(),(actual,oracle.hexdigest())
  assert raw.stat().st_size==size
  assert manifest['run']['result']['raw_result_sha256']==actual
  if name=='small':
   expected=(json.dumps({'softwarecheck':True,'parameters':parameters,'checks':checks,'cases':[normalized]*N},sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode()
   assert raw.read_bytes()==expected
  record.update(correctness='pass exact independent canonical SHA256/size and manifest binding'+('; full bytes equality' if name=='small' else ''),raw_bytes=size,raw_sha256=actual,status='pass')
 else:
  wall,cpu=time.monotonic(),time.process_time()
  try:checked=core.check_artifacts(directory)
  finally:record.update(wall_seconds=time.monotonic()-wall,cpu_seconds=time.process_time()-cpu,native_peak_rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss)
  assert checked['envelope_valid'] and not checked['scientific_validation']
  with (directory/'raw-result.json').open() as f:parsed=json.load(f)
  assert len(parsed['cases'])==N
  assert all(parsed['cases'][i]==normalized for i in (0,N//2,N-1))
  record.update(status='pass',correctness='core envelope/hash check plus parsed count and first/middle/last exact rows',raw_bytes=(directory/'raw-result.json').stat().st_size)
except BaseException as error:
 record.update(status='fail',exception=type(error).__name__,message=str(error),native_peak_rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss,partial_artifacts={p.name:p.stat().st_size for p in directory.glob('*')})
 traceback.print_exc()
 print(json.dumps(record,sort_keys=True),flush=True)
 (OUT/(mode+'-'+variant+'-'+name+'.measurement.json')).write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
 sys.exit(1)
print(json.dumps(record,sort_keys=True),flush=True)
(OUT/(mode+'-'+variant+'-'+name+'.measurement.json')).write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')

RECONSTRUCTION INPUT retain_artifacts.py
import base64,gzip,hashlib,json
from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path('/tmp/lattice-serialization-source-282fd3')
records=[]
for directory in ('baseline-small','candidate-small','candidate-headroom','io-failure-observed'):
 for name in ('raw-result.json','manifest.yaml'):
  original=OUT/directory/name
  if not original.exists():continue
  compressed=OUT/(directory+'-'+name+'.gz')
  raw_digest=hashlib.sha256();raw_size=0
  with original.open('rb') as src, compressed.open('xb') as dst:
   with gzip.GzipFile(filename='',mode='wb',fileobj=dst,mtime=0) as encoded:
    for chunk in iter(lambda:src.read(1024*1024),b''):
     raw_digest.update(chunk);raw_size+=len(chunk);encoded.write(chunk)
  encoded_bytes=compressed.read_bytes()
  records.append({'original_path':str(original),'raw_bytes':raw_size,'raw_sha256':raw_digest.hexdigest(),'gzip_path':str(compressed),'gzip_bytes':len(encoded_bytes),'gzip_sha256':hashlib.sha256(encoded_bytes).hexdigest(),'compression':'gzip deterministic header mtime0, copied actual original artifact in1MiB chunks outside timed controls','gzip_base64':base64.b64encode(encoded_bytes).decode('ascii')})
(OUT/'retained-artifacts.json').write_text(json.dumps({'schema':'lattice-ic.actual-software-artifact-retention.v1','artifacts':records},indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps([{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k!='gzip_base64'} for r in records],indent=2))

RECONSTRUCTION INPUT verify_retention.py
import base64,gzip,hashlib,io,json
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/tmp/lattice-serialization-source-282fd3')
for a in json.loads((root/'retained-artifacts.json').read_text())['artifacts']:
 encoded=base64.b64decode(a['gzip_base64'],validate=True)
 assert len(encoded)==a['gzip_bytes']
 assert hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()==a['gzip_sha256']
 actual=hashlib.sha256();size=0
 with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=io.BytesIO(encoded),mode='rb') as reader:
  for chunk in iter(lambda:reader.read(1024*1024),b''):
   actual.update(chunk);size+=len(chunk)
 assert actual.hexdigest()==a['raw_sha256'] and size==a['raw_bytes']
 print(json.dumps({'path':a['original_path'],'restored_raw_sha256':actual.hexdigest(),'restored_raw_bytes':size,'status':'pass'}))

from pathlib import Path
OUT=Path('/tmp/lattice-serialization-source-282fd3')
paths=[OUT/d/n for d in ('candidate-v2-small','candidate-v2-headroom') for n in ('raw-result.json','manifest.yaml')]
paths += [OUT/'v1'/n for n in ('software-insight.json','transcript.txt','lattice_ic_core.py','test_lattice_ic_core.py','preservation-manifest.json')]
paths += [OUT/n for n in ('frozen-fixture.json','frozen-metadata-note.json','stress.py','guard.py','record.py')]

 compressed=OUT/('retained-v2-'+str(original.relative_to(OUT)).replace('/','_')+'.gz')

 with original.open('rb') as src, compressed.open('xb') as dst:
  with gzip.GzipFile(filename='',mode='wb',fileobj=dst,mtime=0) as encoded:
   for chunk in iter(lambda:src.read(1024*1024),b''):
    raw_digest.update(chunk);raw_size+=len(chunk);encoded.write(chunk)
 encoded_bytes=compressed.read_bytes()
 records.append({'original_path':str(original),'raw_bytes':raw_size,'raw_sha256':raw_digest.hexdigest(),'gzip_path':str(compressed),'gzip_bytes':len(encoded_bytes),'gzip_sha256':hashlib.sha256(encoded_bytes).hexdigest(),'compression':'gzip deterministic header mtime0, copied actual original artifact in1MiB chunks outside timed controls','gzip_base64':base64.b64encode(encoded_bytes).decode('ascii')})
(OUT/'retained-artifacts-v2.json').write_text(json.dumps({'schema':'lattice-ic.actual-software-artifact-retention.v1','artifacts':records},indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps([{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k!='gzip_base64'} for r in records],indent=2))

RECONSTRUCTION INPUT verify_retention_v2.py
import base64,gzip,hashlib,io,json
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('/tmp/lattice-serialization-source-282fd3')
for a in json.loads((root/'retained-artifacts-v2.json').read_text())['artifacts']:
 encoded=base64.b64decode(a['gzip_base64'],validate=True)
 assert len(encoded)==a['gzip_bytes']
 assert hashlib.sha256(encoded).hexdigest()==a['gzip_sha256']
 actual=hashlib.sha256();size=0
 with gzip.GzipFile(fileobj=io.BytesIO(encoded),mode='rb') as reader:
  for chunk in iter(lambda:reader.read(1024*1024),b''):
   actual.update(chunk);size+=len(chunk)
 assert actual.hexdigest()==a['raw_sha256'] and size==a['raw_bytes']
 print(json.dumps({'path':a['original_path'],'restored_raw_sha256':actual.hexdigest(),'restored_raw_bytes':size,'status':'pass'}))

  row={'exact':Fraction(-7,13),'path':Path('synthetic/é'),'tuple':(2**160,-2**161,None,True,-0.0),'nested':[{}, {}, {}, {}, {}, {}]}
  result={'softwarecheck':True,'parameters':parameters,'checks':checks,'cases':[row]*N}
  ballast=bytearray(fixture['resident_ballast_bytes'])
  wall,cpu=time.monotonic(),time.process_time()
  try:
   manifest=core.write_artifacts(result,directory,7,datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),wall,cpu)
  finally:
   record.update(wall_seconds=time.monotonic()-wall,cpu_seconds=time.process_time()-cpu,native_peak_rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss)
  del ballast,result;gc.collect()
  raw=directory/'raw-result.json'
  # Independent canonical oracle: literal scaffolding from stdlib normalized
  # small object, and one separately rendered row repeated, no candidate encoder.
  row_bytes=('\n'.join('    '+line for line in json.dumps(normalized,sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False).splitlines())).encode()
  outer=(json.dumps({'softwarecheck':True,'parameters':parameters,'checks':checks,'cases':[]},sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode()
  prefix,suffix=outer.split(b'[]',1)
  oracle=hashlib.sha256();size=0
  def feed(b):
   global size
   oracle.update(b);size+=len(b)
  feed(prefix+b'[\n')
  for i in range(N):
   if i:feed(b',\n')
   feed(row_bytes)
  feed(b'\n  ]'+suffix)
  actual=core._file_sha256(raw) if hasattr(core,'_file_sha256') else hashlib.file_digest(raw.open('rb'),'sha256').hexdigest()
  assert actual==oracle.hexdigest(),(actual,oracle.hexdigest())
  assert raw.stat().st_size==size
  assert manifest['run']['result']['raw_result_sha256']==actual
  if name=='small':
   expected=(json.dumps({'softwarecheck':True,'parameters':parameters,'checks':checks,'cases':[normalized]*N},sort_keys=True,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode()
   assert raw.read_bytes()==expected
  record.update(correctness='pass exact independent canonical SHA256/size and manifest binding'+('; full bytes equality' if name=='small' else ''),raw_bytes=size,raw_sha256=actual,status='pass')
 else:
  wall,cpu=time.monotonic(),time.process_time()
  try:checked=core.check_artifacts(directory)
  finally:record.update(wall_seconds=time.monotonic()-wall,cpu_seconds=time.process_time()-cpu,native_peak_rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss)
  assert checked['envelope_valid'] and not checked['scientific_validation']
  with (directory/'raw-result.json').open() as f:parsed=json.load(f)
  assert len(parsed['cases'])==N
  assert all(parsed['cases'][i]==normalized for i in (0,N//2,N-1))
  record.update(status='pass',correctness='core envelope/hash check plus parsed count and first/middle/last exact rows',raw_bytes=(directory/'raw-result.json').stat().st_size)
except BaseException as error:
 record.update(status='fail',exception=type(error).__name__,message=str(error),native_peak_rss=resource.getrusage(resource.RUSAGE_SELF).ru_maxrss,partial_artifacts={p.name:p.stat().st_size for p in directory.glob('*')})
 traceback.print_exc()
 print(json.dumps(record,sort_keys=True),flush=True)
 (OUT/(mode+'-'+variant+'-'+name+'.measurement.json')).write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
 sys.exit(1)
print(json.dumps(record,sort_keys=True),flush=True)
(OUT/(mode+'-'+variant+'-'+name+'.measurement.json')).write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')

END ORIGINAL stress.py

import json, os, subprocess, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path('/workspace/lattice-index-calculus')
OUT = Path('/tmp/lattice-serialization-source-282fd3')
name, *command = sys.argv[1:]
started = time.time()
with (OUT/(name+'.stdout')).open('wb') as stdout, (OUT/(name+'.stderr')).open('wb') as stderr:
    proc = subprocess.run(command, cwd=ROOT, stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, env={**os.environ, 'OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS':'1', 'TMPDIR':str(OUT), 'PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE':'1'})
record = {'name':name,'argv':command,'cwd':str(ROOT),'returncode':proc.returncode,'wall_seconds':time.time()-started,'stdout':str(OUT/(name+'.stdout')),'stderr':str(OUT/(name+'.stderr'))}
with (OUT/'commands.jsonl').open('a') as f: f.write(json.dumps(record)+'\n')
print(json.dumps(record))
sys.exit(proc.returncode)
